[Icemule Trace, Town Center] (4042150)
A bustling square dusted with snow sprawls beneath a sturdy mule statue standing placidly atop a tall plinth of silver-veined black stone.  The sun filters through the ice of the statue, showering the throng with a kaleidoscope of rainbowed shards.  Some denizens, mostly halflings but with a scattering of other folk mixed in, chatter by a roaring bonfire, lazing on a smooth dark stone bench or reclining on colorful cloaks spread out on the ground.  Others mill around a burled pine message board standing close to a colorful silver-bound barrel.  You also see a sharp-beaked drake verlok'ar that is flying around, the white Bippo disk, the Ankerath disk, the Saurost disk, the Glennroy disk, the icy blue Besrudio disk, the green Arkahn disk, the Tauxyn disk, the Valandryn disk, a joyous forest spirit that is flying around, a soft grey penguin feather, a blue-eyed charcoal cat, a long-haired larch caterpillar-shaped chocolate, a white chocolate empath, a white chocolate empath, a white chocolate empath, a white chocolate empath and a woven bark wastebin.
Also here: Bippo, Zinnanzi, Ankerath, Saurost, Glennroy, Journeyman Besrudio, Arkahn, Eternil, Tauxyn, Lord Valandryn, Noble Cranbesh
Obvious paths: north, northeast, east, south, west

Oddly undisturbed, a thin marble shelf to the left of the opening holds a tall slender vase, which is adorned with a dried garland of wild roses around its slender neck.  A flickering torch has been driven into the ground below it, though its light seems uncertain and barely throws back the darkness beyond the breach.
Obvious exits: north

[Icemule Trace, Town Center - 2300] (u4042150)
A bustling square dusted with snow sprawls beneath a sturdy mule statue standing placidly atop a tall plinth of silver-veined black stone.  The sun filters through the ice of the statue, showering the throng with a kaleidoscope of rainbowed shards.  Some denizens, mostly halflings but with a scattering of other folk mixed in, chatter by a roaring bonfire, lazing on a smooth dark stone bench or reclining on colorful cloaks spread out on the ground.  Others mill around a burled pine message board standing close to a colorful silver-bound barrel.  You also see the discordant Siarnaq disk, a flying brown leather tome, the Tauxyn disk, the white Bippo disk, the Glennroy disk, the Ankerath disk, a golden verlok feather, the Chamorr disk, a chubby honey blonde puppy with shaggy fur, a comical glacier spirit that is flying around, a pumpkin orange tabby kitten, the Demitone disk, a grey wolf that is sitting, a golden verlok feather, a golden verlok feather, a soft grey penguin feather, a blue-eyed charcoal cat, a long-haired larch caterpillar-shaped chocolate, a white chocolate empath, a white chocolate empath and a woven bark wastebin.
